The wallet computes the consensus preimage itself, obtains consent covering the actual commitment, atomically reserves the input and returns DER low-S signature bytes without a sighash byte. The application appends the requested flag when constructing the unlocking script. A different consensus-valid signature nonce may produce different DER bytes; the digest and successful verification are the conformance criteria. Revalidation on later wallet completion cannot constrain an external party holding this signature.

The envelope’s JSON bytes are not the signed message. Verification does not itself authorize spending or reserve an input. Appending a script sighash byte to the DER-only API signature would also change the API input and is not required here.

### A.66. Duplicate JSON members

The data bytes contain two version members. The duplication is malformed even though the values agree.

An application originates this request to the conforming wallet:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": true,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMDY2",
  "call": "createSignature",
  "args": {
    "protocolID": [
      2,
      "p mandala issue 41513d3d 41673d3d"
    ],
    "keyID": "mandala",
    "counterparty": "anyone",
    "data": [123,34,118,101,114,115,105,111,110,34,58,49,44,34,118,101,114,115,105,111,110,34,58,49,125],
    "seekPermission": true
  }
}
```

The wallet returns:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": false,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMDY2",
  "status": "error",
  "description": "Duplicate member in Mandala metadata",
  "code": 6
}
```

The parser detects duplication before treating the JSON object as a map. The same rule applies at every nested level, including remittances, operations and constraints. Missing required members also prevent signing.

### A.68. Reject invalid byte-array elements

The wallet returns:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": false,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMDY4",
  "status": "error",
  "description": "Invalid transaction byte array",
  "code": 6
```

Strings, base64, numeric-key objects, booleans, fractions and empty tx arrays likewise cannot stand in for the required nonempty byte array. Bounds and encoding are checked before parsing or allocation; no truncation or modulo conversion is permitted.

### A.69. Reject malformed UTF-8

The data bytes are not valid UTF-8 and therefore cannot encode a request envelope.

An application originates this request to the conforming wallet:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": true,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMDY5",
  "call": "createSignature",
  "args": {
    "protocolID": [
      2,
      "p mandala issue 41513d3d 41673d3d"
    ],
    "keyID": "mandala",
    "counterparty": "anyone",
    "data": [195,40],
    "seekPermission": true
  }
}
```

The wallet returns:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": false,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMDY5",
  "status": "error",
  "description": "Mandala envelope is not strict UTF-8",
  "code": 6
}
```

No replacement-character decoding is allowed. Plan hex must likewise be lowercase, even-length and complete, with 200 hex characters in every nonfinal chunk.

### A.70. Reject count overflow before allocation

The declared plan part count exceeds uint32. The wallet must not allocate based on this untrusted count.

An application originates this request to the conforming wallet:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": true,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMDcw",
  "call": "createAction",
  "args": {
    "description": "Pay 300 token units to Bob",
    "outputs": [
      {
        "lockingScript": "205871cf3d1e0d41aa96e94f19686bbb2c7c96b84afd9d73cbacdff00596be104f022c016d76a914d70bcb7e47c238c22be672040bd7565c5f003bc688ac",
        "satoshis": 1,
        "outputDescription": "Bob receives 300 units"
      }
    ],
    "labels": [
      "p mandala plan 0 4294967296 aa"
    ],
    "version": 1,
    "lockTime": 0,
    "options": {
      "randomizeOutputs": false,
      "returnTXIDOnly": true,
      "acceptDelayedBroadcast": false
    }
  }
}
```

The wallet returns:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": false,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMDcw",
  "status": "error",
  "description": "Mandala plan count is outside uint32 bounds",
  "code": 6
}
```

No signature is released and provisional reservations are rolled back. No token credit or settled debit is recorded.

```json
      "signAndProcess": true,
      "acceptDelayedBroadcast": false,
      "noSend": true
    }
  }
}
```

The wallet returns:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": false,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMDcy",
  "status": "success",
  "result": {

    ]
  }

```

The returned transaction ID and ordinary change outpoint identify a locally completed action, not an admitted token receipt. No token topic is submitted yet. The signed capability and managed inputs remain recorded, and the prospective 700-unit token change is not made spendable solely because Bitcoin serialization and signatures are complete.

### A.73. Send a previously prepared action

The transfer from A.72 is retained by the wallet and has not conflicted. This request uses the ordinary batch-only createAction flow.

An application originates this request to the conforming wallet:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": true,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMDcz",
  "call": "createAction",
  "args": {
    "description": "Send prepared Mandala transfer",
    "options": {
      "sendWith": [
        "3583d165f81779b11824c487bf604a8f7492faabf0ee17e69aeaa9c4aca35bd4"
      ],
      "returnTXIDOnly": true,
      "acceptDelayedBroadcast": false
    }
  }
}
```

The wallet returns:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": false,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMDcz",
  "status": "success",
  "result": {
    "sendWithResults": [
      {
        "txid": "3583d165f81779b11824c487bf604a8f7492faabf0ee17e69aeaa9c4aca35bd4",
        "status": "sending"
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

The wallet resolves the saved plan and authorization, rechecks current token state, and processes the action on the relevant token topic along with Bitcoin submission. The result uses the existing sendWithResults shape; sending does not itself claim mining or topic admission. The wallet records subsequent admission before crediting change. It neither requires invented new inputs/outputs nor charges the same authorized transfer again.

### A.74. Abort after releasing a signature

Start with the pending offer from A.47, including Alice’s already released signature. The wallet cancels its own pending processing.

An application originates this request to the conforming wallet:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": true,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMDc0",
  "call": "abortAction",
  "args": {
    "reference": "bWFuZGFsYS1zd2FwLTAwMDE="
  }
}
```

The wallet returns:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": false,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMDc0",
  "status": "success",
  "result": {
    "aborted": true
  }
}
```

Cancellation does not retract Alice’s signature. The wallet retains the exposure record and does not immediately offer A to another conflicting action. It can free that exposure after an authenticated completion or conflict makes the capability unusable, or after explicit consent to a conflicting spend that invalidates the old offer. BRC-177 expiry likewise cannot revoke bytes another party holds.

This is the same authorization record, not a second swap. The wallet does not deduct another allowance, create another credit, or attempt to select another A input. The CWI message ID is only transport correlation; the saved action reference and wallet action state identify this retry.

### A.90. Action-label queries retain their matching meaning

No saved action has the selected plan label in this isolated read fixture. The wallet authorizes this origin to query its actions.

An application originates this request to the conforming wallet:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": true,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMDkw",
  "call": "listActions",
  "args": {
    "labels": [
      "p mandala plan 0 5 7b2276657273696f6e223a312c22617373657473223a5b22346631306265393630356630646661636362373339646664346162383936376332636262366236383139346665393936616134313064316533646366373135385f30225d2c22696e70757454"
    ],
    "labelQueryMode": "all",
    "limit": 10,
    "offset": 0
  }
}
```

The wallet returns:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": false,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMDkw",
  "status": "success",
  "result": {
    "totalActions": 0,
    "actions": []
  }
}
```

The label is an ordinary exact query term at this boundary, not an instruction to reconstruct and execute a new plan. A wallet applies module-aware read permissions and returns the usual action list shape.

The wallet records the precise authorized exposure and reserves A before releasing the signature. It does not credit B while the transaction remains partial. Every consensus-valid completion using this signature retains the named input outpoints and outputs; a conflicting spend of the named B UTXO prevents this offer from completing. This is transaction-level commitment, not a guarantee that either overlay will later admit the output.

### A.95. Decline an unguaranteed swap

Alice permits only an offer whose named incoming B source and B receipt remain binding. The request instead uses ALL|FORKID|ANYONECANPAY, and no independent covenant or other commitment binds that incoming source.

### A.96. Conditional consideration with a verified remedy

The proposed layout is the same swap, but Alice's wallet has independently verified an applicable asset-ecosystem remedy under its owner-configured policy. That remedy covers the loss of her A contribution if the unbound B source does not supply admitted consideration. The fixture deliberately does not instantiate or standardize the remedy. Alice consents to the precisely described conditional capability, including its limits; disclosure alone would not be enough to authorize it.

The wallet records the precise authorized exposure and reserves A before releasing the signature. It does not credit B while the transaction remains partial. The wallet explains that the signature does not bind the incoming B source, that overlay admission remains conditional, and which independently verified remedy protects Alice if consideration fails. It neither claims hashPrevouts coverage nor relies on future signAction revalidation to constrain an externally completed signature. Without that remedy the required result is A.95's refusal.


Even a matching wallet key and a known overlay history cannot turn the burn record into value. It is excluded from incoming-value netting, value selection and managed value baskets. Both the explicit burn and any implicit remainder burn require the creator’s consent; neither is an incoming payment.

The wallet fails explicitly before large allocation or signing; it does not truncate the transaction, omit evidence, or partially decode a plan. The module’s 16 MiB ceiling still applies when the host supports larger requests. Lower plan and proof resource limits must likewise be explicit failures, never silent omission.

The wallet retains the real completed transaction 3583d165f81779b11824c487bf604a8f7492faabf0ee17e69aeaa9c4aca35bd4 and reports its unresolved token processing state. It does not pretend Bitcoin completion was rolled back or mark the 700-unit change spendable. A later admission retry reuses this action and does not create a second debit or transaction. Multi-asset processing must similarly retain each topic’s individual result; one successful topic does not authorize credits on the others.

```json
      "tx": [1,1,1,1,212,91,163,172,196,169,234,154,230,23,238,240,171,250,146,116,143,74,96,191,135,196,36,24,177,121,23,248,101,209,131,53,2,0,190,239,0,2,2,88,113,207,61,30,13,65,170,150,233,79,25,104,107,187,44,124,150,184,74,253,157,115,203,172,223,240,5,150,190,16,79,0,1,0,0,0,1,88,113,207,61,30,13,65,170,150,233,79,25,104,107,187,44,124,150,184,74,253,157,115,203,172,223,240,5,150,190,16,79,0,0,0,0,107,72,48,69,2,33,0,133,4,241,188,67,131,30,102,166,181,51,117,200,62,49,183,106,182,15,227,186,31,180,191,26,250,32,169,49,68,4,200,2,32,83,221,113,121,178,229,147,197,107,204,10,88,207,147,153,159,78,29,89,134,89,78,172,73,217,56,110,141,79,233,121,217,65,33,2,199,206,13,249,125,252,54,54,59,6,171,35,230,87,245,203,239,132,119,73,161,238,102,84,229,176,181,41,30,42,96,186,255,255,255,255,3,1,0,0,0,0,0,0,0,62,32,88,113,207,61,30,13,65,170,150,233,79,25,104,107,187,44,124,150,184,74,253,157,115,203,172,223,240,5,150,190,16,79,2,44,1,109,118,169,20,215,11,203,126,71,194,56,194,43,230,114,4,11,215,86,92,95,0,59,198,136,172,1,0,0,0,0,0,0,0,62,32,88,113,207,61,30,13,65,170,150,233,79,25,104,107,187,44,124,150,184,74,253,157,115,203,172,223,240,5,150,190,16,79,2,188,2,109,118,169,20,142,102,21,226,208,156,202,206,154,178,122,104,203,109,7,87,229,31,218,231,136,172,220,3,0,0,0,0,0,0,25,118,169,20,150,247,198,175,245,135,77,44,87,248,202,117,70,109,54,32,255,251,155,196,136,172,0,0,0,0],

```

The pending reference remains valid for a later signAction with an empty spends object, because no caller script remains to be supplied. The wallet still refreshes overlay state and consent before processing. Returning fully populated scripts inside a pending action does not assert admission or release its reservations.

### A.104. Process a managed-only pending action

Start with the prepared action in A.103. Its sources remain current and the recorded permission remains valid. No new unlocking script is needed.

An application originates this request to the conforming wallet:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": true,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMTA0",
  "call": "signAction",
  "args": {
    "reference": "bWFuZGFsYS1wYXktMDAwMDE=",
    "spends": {},
    "options": {
      "returnTXIDOnly": true,
      "acceptDelayedBroadcast": false
    }
  }
}
```

The wallet returns:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": false,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMTA0",
  "status": "success",
  "result": {
    "txid": "3583d165f81779b11824c487bf604a8f7492faabf0ee17e69aeaa9c4aca35bd4"
  }
}
```

The wallet completes the saved action after revalidation and submits it to the A topic. It shares the reservation and authorization already associated with the prepared transaction and does not bill the user’s policy twice.

### A.105. Discover multiple assets without knowing their IDs

This independent inventory contains C reserved by a pending offer, A available, and H available authority. Canonical outpoint ordering puts C before A before H. The origin is authorized to read all three.

The application sends:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": true,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMTA1",
  "call": "listOutputs",
  "args": {
    "basket": "p mandala assets",
    "include": "locking scripts",
    "includeTags": true,
    "limit": 2,
    "offset": 0
  }
}
```

The wallet returns:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": false,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMTA1",
  "status": "success",
  "result": {
    "totalOutputs": 3,
    "outputs": [
      {
        "outpoint": "1f88816c9ff76104618b4ccd3b412d0a0c1ba3d4714667af63b724b5788a36c8.0",
        "satoshis": 1000,
        "lockingScript": "0002b80b6d76a914492fd9b5a20e0ab6977749a9e338dbf7ae076c1b88ac",
        "spendable": false,
        "tags": [
          "mandala_state_reserved"
        ]
      },
      {
        "outpoint": "4f10be9605f0dfaccb739dfd4ab8967c2cbb6b68194fe996aa410d1e3dcf7158.0",
        "satoshis": 1000,
        "lockingScript": "0002e8036d76a914d53cf9be66fc51553c88532d4fa4291270e55ac888ac",
        "spendable": true,
        "tags": [
          "mandala_state_available"
        ]
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

The client derives C and A IDs from the deploy outpoints and scripts. The C record remains visible but is not available for spending. totalOutputs is 3 actual records, not the two records on this page or a sum of their units.

### A.106. Discover an authority-only asset on the next page

The A.105 snapshot has not changed. The client advances by the two records already read.

The application sends:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": true,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMTA2",
  "call": "listOutputs",
  "args": {
    "basket": "p mandala assets",
    "include": "locking scripts",
    "includeTags": true,
    "limit": 2,
    "offset": 2
  }
}
```

The wallet returns:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": false,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMTA2",
  "status": "success",
  "result": {
    "totalOutputs": 3,
    "outputs": [
      {
        "outpoint": "70dae30343936dfce31a61f7becb80770e700f4b0402d2e2cfbc87dded55ee57.0",
        "satoshis": 1000,
        "lockingScript": "00006d76a914ded08688f445a5056c27b144b67df215669f2a5488ac",
        "spendable": true,
        "tags": [
          "mandala_state_available"
        ]
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

The H amount is zero because this is authority. The client lists H with one capability even though it has no fungible value holdings. Every available asset can be discovered without a guessed ID or a global storage basket.

### A.107. BRC-112 token-unit balance

Alice has the available A fixture output, carrying 1000 token units and 1000 satoshis. These happen to coincide in this fixture; the sum is determined by token fields.

The application sends:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": true,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMTA3",
  "call": "listOutputs",
  "args": {
    "basket": "p mandala balance 4f10be9605f0dfaccb739dfd4ab8967c2cbb6b68194fe996aa410d1e3dcf7158v0",
    "limit": 1,
    "offset": 999
  }
}
```

The wallet returns:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": false,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMTA3",
  "status": "success",
  "result": {
    "totalOutputs": 1000,
    "outputs": []
  }
}
```

The module ignores limit and offset and returns the whole available token balance. It omits BEEF and never returns output entries for a balance query. Read permission does not grant permission to sign.

### A.108. Authority balance is a capability count

The available H authority fixture is in its authority basket.

The application sends:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": true,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMTA4",
  "call": "listOutputs",
  "args": {
    "basket": "p mandala balance 70dae30343936dfce31a61f7becb80770e700f4b0402d2e2cfbc87dded55ee57v0 authority"
  }
}
```

The wallet returns:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": false,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMTA4",
  "status": "success",
  "result": {
    "totalOutputs": 1,
    "outputs": []
  }
}
```

The result means one authority, not one token unit or a zero-valued fungible balance. An accessible H value basket with no available value would return zero separately.

### A.109. Reserved value is excluded from available balance

C’s existing value basket contains only the C output reserved in A.105.

The application sends:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": true,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMTA5",
  "call": "listOutputs",
  "args": {
    "basket": "p mandala balance 1f88816c9ff76104618b4ccd3b412d0a0c1ba3d4714667af63b724b5788a36c8v0"
  }
}
```

The wallet returns:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": false,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMTA5",
  "status": "success",
  "result": {
    "totalOutputs": 0,
    "outputs": []
  }
}
```

The inventory still exposes the reserved record to an authorized reader, but an available-balance query excludes it. Failed pre-signature reservations are rolled back; released-signature exposure is retained under the action lifecycle rules.

### A.110. Exact uint64 maximum over JSON

The fixed-supply issuance from A.38 is complete, admitted, unreserved and owned by Alice. Both ends implement the exact module-balance profile.

The application sends:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": true,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMTEw",
  "call": "listOutputs",
  "args": {
    "basket": "p mandala balance b1b1a4a0e643e423699c88874197991ea4cab71e1a60efc361ffe4e07f0aa552v0"
  }
}
```

The wallet returns:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": false,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMTEw",
  "status": "success",
  "result": {
    "totalOutputs": "18446744073709551615",
    "outputs": []
  }
}
```

The quoted JSON decimal string is canonical for this range. The receiving JavaScript adapter exposes totalOutputs as 18446744073709551615n, without first converting it to Number. The binary totalOutputs field is nine ff bytes: the ff marker and eight unsigned ff payload bytes; it is not the optional-field -1 sentinel.

### A.111. Largest safe JavaScript numeric balance

In this independent H state, valid authority issuance has produced an available value total of exactly 9007199254740991 units.

The application sends:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": true,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMTEx",
  "call": "listOutputs",
  "args": {
    "basket": "p mandala balance 70dae30343936dfce31a61f7becb80770e700f4b0402d2e2cfbc87dded55ee57v0"
  }
}
```

The wallet returns:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": false,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMTEx",
  "status": "success",
  "result": {
    "totalOutputs": 9007199254740991,
    "outputs": []
  }
}
```

The API and JSON response use a number at this boundary. The next integer, 9007199254740992, must use bigint at the JavaScript API and a decimal string in JSON. Clients reject noncanonical strings for the small range and unsafe JSON numeric values.

### A.112. Aggregate overflow is an explicit error

In this independent H state, two admitted, available value outputs each carry 18446744073709551615 units. Their exact sum is 36893488147419103230.

The application sends:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": true,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMTEy",
  "call": "listOutputs",
  "args": {
    "basket": "p mandala balance 70dae30343936dfce31a61f7becb80770e700f4b0402d2e2cfbc87dded55ee57v0"
  }
}
```

The wallet returns:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": false,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMTEy",
  "status": "error",
  "code": 6,
  "description": "Mandala balance exceeds uint64 response range"
}
```

The holdings are valid and must remain tracked. The wallet computes the full sum before detecting the response bound. It does not round, wrap, return only one output’s amount or force an enumeration fallback.

### A.113. Transport cannot preserve the exact balance

The H balance is 9007199254740993 units, but this wallet adapter cannot emit the exact-integer profile.

The application sends:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": true,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMTEz",
  "call": "listOutputs",
  "args": {
    "basket": "p mandala balance 70dae30343936dfce31a61f7becb80770e700f4b0402d2e2cfbc87dded55ee57v0"
  }
}
```

The wallet returns:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": false,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMTEz",
  "status": "error",
  "code": 2,
  "description": "Exact module balance representation is unsupported"
}
```

The adapter returns an ordinary error rather than casting to Number. An older receiver encountering an unsupported exact result must likewise raise a local protocol error; this example does not claim that all deployed older implementations already enforce that rule.

### A.114. Reject reversed balance prefix

The caller places balance outside the reserved module namespace.

The application sends:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": true,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMTE0",
  "call": "listOutputs",
  "args": {
    "basket": "balance p mandala 4f10be9605f0dfaccb739dfd4ab8967c2cbb6b68194fe996aa410d1e3dcf7158v0"
  }
}
```

The wallet returns:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": false,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMTE0",
  "status": "error",
  "code": 6,
  "description": "Balance prefix must follow the module identifier"
}
```

The wallet does not bypass module dispatch or normal read permission checks. The supported order is p mandala balance followed by the asset tag.

### A.115. Discovery permission is required

This origin lacks inventory read permission and forbids an interactive request.

The application sends:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": true,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMTE1",
  "call": "listOutputs",
  "args": {
    "basket": "p mandala assets",
    "include": "locking scripts",
    "includeTags": true,
    "limit": 2,
    "offset": 0,
    "seekPermission": false
  }
}
```

The wallet returns:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": false,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMTE1",
  "status": "error",
  "code": 1,
  "description": "Mandala inventory read permission is required"
}
```

No output, asset ID or count is disclosed. A generic cryptographic or spending grant does not automatically authorize this inventory scope.

### A.117. Pending admission is visible without spendable credit

The transfer from A.14 has completed on Bitcoin, consuming A’s source, but its generated 700-unit change is awaiting overlay admission. This independent inventory has only that locally controlled pending record.

The application sends:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": true,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMTE3",
  "call": "listOutputs",
  "args": {
    "basket": "p mandala assets",
    "include": "locking scripts",
    "includeTags": true,
    "limit": 10,
    "offset": 0
  }
}
```

The wallet returns:

```json
{
  "type": "CWI",
  "isInvocation": false,
  "id": "bWFuZGFsYTAwMTE3",
  "status": "success",
  "result": {
    "totalOutputs": 1,
    "outputs": [
      {
        "outpoint": "3583d165f81779b11824c487bf604a8f7492faabf0ee17e69aeaa9c4aca35bd4.1",
        "satoshis": 1,
        "lockingScript": "205871cf3d1e0d41aa96e94f19686bbb2c7c96b84afd9d73cbacdff00596be104f02bc026d76a9148e6615e2d09ccace9ab27a68cb6d0757e51fdae788ac",
        "spendable": false,
        "tags": [
          "mandala_state_pending"
        ]
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

The client may display 700 prospective units with pending status. It must not display them as available or resurrect the spent source. The available-balance query is zero until the required admission checks succeed.

### A.118. Cached proof with a wrong scope cannot justify receipt

The request is the valid compact swap receipt from A.77. Historical B admission evidence is unavailable, and the wallet's only cached packet covers the unrelated C asset, `1f88816c9ff76104618b4ccd3b412d0a0c1ba3d4714667af63b724b5788a36c8_0`, at subject `1f88816c9ff76104618b4ccd3b412d0a0c1ba3d4714667af63b724b5788a36c8.0`. It contains no B token ancestry. The ordinary transaction sources and current received-output membership are authenticated, but no alternative B historical admission or complete lineage evidence is available.

The application sends:

The wallet returns:

```json

  "isInvocation": false,

```

The wallet rejects after validating the cached packet’s scope. Removing proof bytes from the request does not weaken BRC-176 completeness, asset binding or subject binding. A positive current receipt lookup alone does not replace missing historical or lineage evidence.

## Appendix B — Exact transition and safety vectors

These vectors supplement Appendix A. Amounts are integer token units for
one existing asset, with authenticated admitted inputs and individually valid
encoded outputs. All non-token funding, scripts, source proofs and current
topic checks pass unless a row says otherwise. A successful row requires
exactly the listed operation set in its plan and consent to each positive
quantity; an omitted or extra operation fails before signing. An invalid row
fails before signing regardless of declared operations. An empty operation set
does not authorize a token-bearing action without its exact `assets` entry.

### B.1. Mixed-encoding value accounting

`V`, `T`, `E`, `M` and `B` have the definitions in Transaction evidence and
admission. `A` is the number of admitted authority inputs and outputs.
`X/Y/Z` is the resulting `transferred/minted/burned` triple. Each row is a
separate transaction; no value or authority carries between rows. `tr`,
`mi`, `bu` and `at` abbreviate `transfer`, `mint`, `burn` and
`authority-transfer` only within this table.

| Case | V | T | E | M | B | A in→out | X/Y/Z | Required operations or result |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | --- | --- | --- |
| B01 exact binary transfer | 100 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 100 | 0→0 | 100/0/0 | tr |
| B02 implicit binary burn | 100 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 90 | 0→0 | 90/0/10 | tr, bu |
| B03 no-authority binary excess | 100 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 101 | 0→0 | — | Reject; binary outputs lack coverage even though inputs would be destroyed by an invalid transition. |
| B04 legacy plus binary transfer | 100 | 40 | 0 | 0 | 60 | 0→0 | 100/0/0 | tr |
| B05 mixed over-output | 100 | 70 | 0 | 0 | 40 | 0→0 | — | Reject; remaining value covers only 30 binary units. |
| B06 explicit plus implicit burn | 100 | 20 | 10 | 0 | 60 | 0→0 | 80/0/20 | tr, bu |
| B07 explicit burn exhausts remainder | 100 | 20 | 10 | 0 | 80 | 0→0 | — | Reject; binary amount exceeds the remaining 70. |
| B08 authority mints binary only | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 250 | 1→1 | 0/250/0 | mi, at |
| B09 authority with value burn | 100 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 90 | 1→1 | 90/0/10 | tr, bu, at; authority does not erase the burn. |
| B10 authority with exact value coverage | 100 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 100 | 1→1 | 100/0/0 | tr, at; `mint` is extra and fails. |
| B11 authority with binary excess | 100 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 140 | 1→1 | 100/40/0 | tr, mi, at |
| B12 mixed, simultaneous mint and burn | 100 | 40 | 10 | 0 | 70 | 1→1 | 90/20/10 | tr, mi, bu, at; net supply rises 10. |
| B13 mixed, remaining value burned | 100 | 40 | 10 | 0 | 30 | 1→1 | 70/0/30 | tr, bu, at |
| B14 legacy mint plus mixed transfer | 100 | 40 | 10 | 30 | 50 | 1→1 | 90/30/10 | tr, mi, bu, at |
| B15 equal legacy mint and burn | 100 | 0 | 100 | 100 | 0 | 1→0 | 0/100/100 | mi, bu, authority-end; net zero does not cancel either consent. |
| B16 authority cannot fund legacy transfer | 0 | 1 | 0 | 100 | 0 | 1→1 | — | Reject; `T + E > V` despite authority. |
| B17 legacy mint only | 0 | 0 | 0 | 100 | 0 | 1→1 | 0/100/0 | mi, at; no `transfer`. |
| B18 legacy transfer plus binary mint | 100 | 100 | 0 | 0 | 100 | 1→1 | 100/100/0 | tr, mi, at; the same value input cannot fund both outputs. |
| B19 authority-free legacy mint | 0 | 0 | 0 | 1 | 0 | 0→0 | — | Reject; `mint` requires an admitted authority input. |
| B20 wide aggregate | 36893488147419103230 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 36893488147419103230 | 0→0 | 36893488147419103230/0/0 | tr; two maximum-uint64 inputs and outputs, summed exactly without uint64 overflow. |

For B20 each input and output amount is 18446744073709551615;
the table totals deliberately exceed uint64. A single encoded output above
uint64 remains invalid. If B16's legacy mint is independently admissible under
BRC-161, that does not make its legacy transfer valid: the wallet still rejects
the action because the intended transition is not wholly admitted.

### B.2. Authority and operation-set boundaries

All rows have a single existing asset, no value amounts, valid authority
scripts and separately verified controller consent. The operation is in
addition to any positive value operation from B.1; count changes never turn
authority into numeric value.

| Case | Admitted authority inputs | Authority outputs | Required result |
| --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
| B21 end one | 1 | 0 | `authority-end` |
| B22 transfer one | 1 | 1 | `authority-transfer`, even if the controller changes |
| B23 split one | 1 | 2 | `authority-split` |
| B24 end several | 2 | 0 | `authority-end` |
| B25 combine | 2 | 1 | `authority-combine` |
| B26 same count | 2 | 2 | `authority-transfer`, including swaps of controllers |
| B27 net split | 2 | 3 | `authority-split` |
| B28 unauthorized creation | 0 | 1 | Reject; no authority input |
| B29 absent capability | 0 | 0 | No authority operation; an asserted one is extra and fails. |

A fixed or authority deploy is instead its new asset's sole deploy operation.
The BRC-162 deploy must occupy output zero. Its genesis amount or authority
is not charged as an existing-asset transfer or mint. A BRC-161 deploy follows
that encoding's own allowed position. A binary prefix takes precedence over
an embedded legacy inscription; its legacy text cannot add a second amount or
operation. Assets are evaluated independently: a surplus of asset A cannot
cover a deficit of asset B.

### B.3. Signature commitment and provider boundaries

The signature rows assume consensus-valid source scripts and otherwise
authorized unilateral disposition. `nIn` and `nOut` are transaction input
and output counts. Both direct signing and managed-action signing MUST produce
the same accept/reject decision for the stated context. A rejection releases
no signature or pending signature capability.

| Case | Flag | Signing input | nIn/nOut | Required result |
| --- | --- | ---: | --- | --- |
| B30 BIP143 SINGLE matched | `0x43` | 0 | 1/1 | May sign; commits output zero after ordinary validation. |
| B31 BIP143 SINGLE absent | `0x43` | 1 | 2/1 | Reject; no corresponding output, even if that digest algorithm uses zero `hashOutputs`. |
| B32 BIP143 SINGLE/ANYONECANPAY absent | `0xc3` | 1 | 2/1 | Reject; ANYONECANPAY does not cure the missing output. |
| B33 Chronicle SINGLE matched | `0x63` | 0 | 1/1 | May sign using the original transaction digest algorithm, not BIP143 fields. |
| B34 Chronicle SINGLE absent | `0x63` | 1 | 2/1 | Reject; the original algorithm's missing-output result is the constant uint256 value 1. |
| B35 Chronicle SINGLE/ANYONECANPAY absent | `0xe3` | 1 | 2/1 | Reject for the same reason; no outpoint or output is committed by the degenerate digest. |
| B36 NONE with no outputs committed | `0x42` | 0 | 1/1 | May sign only if its actual unilateral consequences or a verified safeguard satisfy authorization; the output shown in a subject is not promised. |
| B37 ALL/ANYONECANPAY conditional swap | `0xc1` | 0 | 2/2 | Reject when a named foreign input is required consideration but uncommitted and no independently verified remedy applies. |
| B38 ALL/ANYONECANPAY with remedy | `0xc1` | 0 | 2/2 | May sign only after the wallet verifies an applicable remedy under the asset system's policy; the remedy is outside this BRC. |

For B31–B35 the subject and source may otherwise be valid; missing output
coverage alone triggers rejection. The actual signed message is computed from
the selected consensus algorithm, never from a caller-supplied digest.
SIGHASH_SINGLE's constant-one result is a consensus property, not a permissible
wallet signature vector.

| Case | Evidence or policy condition | Required result |
| --- | --- | --- |
| B39 no caller hint | Authenticated token source with no `inputTopics` member | Derive asset, topic and configured provider from the source; continue only if current admission passes. |
| B40 caller topic hint | Otherwise valid plan or signature envelope contains `inputTopics` | Reject as an unknown version-1 member; do not let it select a topic. |
| B41 differing wallet policies | Provider P admits the source; P is trusted by wallet A but wallet B requires other evidence | A may proceed under its configuration; B fails closed until its own configured rule is satisfied. Neither wallet can deem the other's policy authoritative. |
| B42 issuer convention mismatch | Source is admitted by one wallet, but intended counterparty requires a different asset-network convention | Do not promise counterparty acceptance; apply the wallet's asset and counterparty policy before release. |
| B43 provider disagrees or is stale | Required current evidence is insufficient under this wallet's configuration | Reject before signature or broadcast; an old proof cannot substitute for current spendability. |
| B44 delayed actor | A later untrusted participant omits a required signature or changes an uncommitted output | No loss beyond the releasing user's unconditional authorized disposition, or a verified system-specific remedy must cover the conditional loss; otherwise reject the initial release. |
| B45 unrelated controller | Alice releases a valid signature for her input; Bob's separately controlled input remains unsigned | Alice's signature cannot spend Bob's input or invalidate Bob's independently admitted asset. |

These cases set wallet safety and evidence boundaries. Provider choice,
facilitator functions, negotiation, participation authorization, acceptance
conventions and remedies remain choices of the applicable asset ecosystem,
issuer, wallet, application and users, rather than a universal rule imposed by
this BRC.
